# AI-DLC Audit Log

## Workflow Start
**Timestamp**: 2026-11-10T07:41:45Z
**Event**: WORKFLOW_STARTED
**Scope**: feature
**Request**: /aidlc Intent: Learning Journey Build a web app where a learner types any professional topic ("Kubernetes", "data storytelling", "negotiation") and gets back a structured learning journey they can actually follow. What the learner gets: - A journey of 5-6 stages that climbs from fundamentals to practical mastery. - For each stage: a short explanation (what it is, why it matters, what you can do after it), a small set of learning materials (reading, exercises, a mini project, a quiz), and a study plan with time estimates. - A choice of teaching style (persona) before the journey is generated: a friendly guide, a structured instructor, or a planner focused on tracking. - Progress tracking: mark stages complete and see progress update. - Milestones and a completion certificate as they move through the journey. - Goals with a target date, kept visible. - Encouraging nudges when progress stalls (for example, below 50% with a goal date approaching), never guilt-driven. - If AI generation is slow or unavailable, the app falls back to a structured template so the learner still gets a journey. Constraints: - Runs on AWS, serverless, deployed from infrastructure as code in one region. - All AI generation uses Amazon Nova models on Amazon Bedrock only. No other model providers. - Demo scale (tens of users), lowest reasonable cost, easy to tear down. - Web front end, accessible (keyboard navigation, screen-reader friendly). Out of scope for this build: native mobile apps, video generation, multi-language, instructor/admin consoles, payments.
**Source Baseline**: sha256:5b98d808de95fdad048f90cc668d3a04b2e9a7968ea49b5b4532a19302d4ff4d

## Gate Rejected
**Timestamp**: 2026-11-10T15:07:44Z
**Event**: GATE_REJECTED
**Stage**: scope-definition
**Feedback**: 2\nTwo fixes:\n1. Market Research Q7 (D) is missing from scope: add an "AI-generated" label on generated journey content to PU-03, and privacy-aware storage (anonymous session key, no PII, DynamoDB TTL for expiry) to PU-04.\n2. Cut order is inconsistent: your summary says nudges (PU-07) first, the backlog says milestones (PU-06) first. Use nudges first, then milestones/certificate, everywhere.
**Message Id**: f778b914
**Message Ids**: 881bb6e8,f778b914

**Person Reply**: 1 — approve, and fix all eight findings (R-01 to R-08) before moving on. For R-01: the 15-second limit covers all attempts combined; the single retry only starts if at least 5 seconds remain, so the worst case is 15 seconds plus template render, inside NFR2's 20 seconds; add the acceptance case for invalid output at 12 seconds. For R-02: API Gateway throttling of 5 requests per second with a burst of 10 across the whole API; AWS Budgets alerts at 2 dollars of actual daily spend and at 8 dollars for the month, which is 80 percent of the 10 dollar ceiling in NFR4. Apply the reviewer's suggested fixes as written for R-03 to R-08.

Add a centred page container of max-width 960px with 24px side gutters, a proper header bar, consistent padding and spacing inside the stage cards and the summary block, a consistent type scale, and a styled primary button, keeping the existing colours and every accessibility test green. No behaviour changes and no new dependencies. Then re-verify, review, redeploy, re-run check 9, and tag the deploy.
